# Yeti Plateau Cup 300s Triptych Grid Prompts (3x1)

Generated from `prompts/yeti-plateau-cup-300s-3x2.md` by `.agents/skills/generate-triptych-prompts/generate_triptych.mjs`. Do not edit this file by hand: change the panel prompts in the 3x2 file and run the script again.

Each 10-second video is split into two vertical 9:16 triptych grids, each three horizontal 16:9 panels stacked and read top to bottom: Grid 1 holds panels 1 to 3 and Grid 2 holds panels 4 to 6. Generate each grid with the family reference image attached, and check every panel against its panel prompt and the look check in section 7 of `.agents/rules/prompt-assembly.md`, because an image model drawing three panels at once can drift between them.
